# Database Table Classification by Module

**Status:** Complete - Phase 1 Classification Ready for Phase 2
**Last Updated:** 2026-05-09
**Classification Method:** See `step-1_segregation-approach.md` for methodology
**Strategy Context:** `db-optimization-plan-v2.md`

---

## SUMMARY

| Module | REQUIRED | MAYBE | NOT-REQUIRED | Total | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| MCC | 8 | 54 | 26 | 88 | Complete |
| RBS | 28 | 258 | 127 | 413 | Complete |
| SHARED/CROSS | 16 | 387 | 1566 | 1969 | Complete |
| **TOTAL** | **52** | **699** | **1719** | **2470** | **Complete** |

High-level outcome:
- REQUIRED tables are the canonical starting points for Phase 2 schema documentation and parallel discovery bundles.
- MAYBE tables remain available for specialized workflows, historical analysis, derived reporting, and niche operational domains.
- NOT-REQUIRED tables are mainly temp/staging, backup/snapshot, audit/log, bug-fix/test, or entirely different business modules outside MCC/RBS/shared consumer analysis.

---

## MODULE 1: MCC (Metro Customer Care)

**Status:** Complete

This module covers complaint/grievance operations, grievance-side consumer linkage, tanker-related workflows, and complaint-support master data. `MCC_SECT_*` hierarchy materialized views are intentionally classified under `SHARED/CROSS` because they serve all modules.

### REQUIRED Tables

| Table Name | Row Count | Purpose | Primary Use | Typical Join Targets |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| MCC_COMPLAINT | 5,136,156 | Core complaint master transaction table | Reconcile complaint-level operational workflows and grievance detail | MDM_MCC_COMPTYPE, MDM_MCC_COMPREASON, MDM_MCC_GRIEVSTATUS, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER |
| MCC_CONSUMERS_CONNECTIONS | 1,507,744 | Consumer-to-service-connection bridge | Map grievance consumer references to CAN and service attributes | MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER, CONSUMER_ALTERNATE_CONTACT |
| MCC_GRIEVANCES | 6,649,652 | Main grievance transaction table | Filter by date, status, type, consumer, and tanker indicators | MDM_MCC_COMPTYPE, MDM_MCC_COMPREASON, MDM_MCC_GRIEVSTATUS, MDM_MCC_GRIEVRECEIVEDTYPE, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER, MCC_SECT_SUBDIVN_DIVN_CIRCLE_INC_AREAS_MV |
| MDM_MCC_COMPREASON | 59 | Complaint reason lookup | Decode grievance reason codes | (Lookup table) |
| MDM_MCC_COMPTYPE | 10 | Complaint type lookup | Decode grievance type codes to business labels | (Lookup table) |
| MDM_MCC_GRIEVRECEIVEDTYPE | 28 | Complaint source lookup | Decode grievance received/source channels | (Lookup table) |
| MDM_MCC_GRIEVSTATUS | 9 | Grievance status lookup | Decode grievance workflow status | (Lookup table) |
| MDM_MCC_RESOLVINGAUTHORITY | 25 | Resolving authority lookup | Decode resolving authority assignments | (Lookup table) |

### Join Hints for Phase 2 Discovery (MCC Module)

**Use these to batch-query related tables in parallel:**

| Main Table | Typical Join Targets | Why They Are Related |
|---|---|---|
| MCC_GRIEVANCES | MDM_MCC_COMPTYPE, MDM_MCC_COMPREASON, MDM_MCC_GRIEVSTATUS, MDM_MCC_GRIEVRECEIVEDTYPE, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER | Complete grievance detail requires complaint decoding, status decoding, and consumer linkage. |
| MCC_COMPLAINT | MDM_MCC_COMPTYPE, MDM_MCC_COMPREASON, MDM_MCC_GRIEVSTATUS, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN | Alternate complaint master with the same core decoding needs as the canonical grievance table. |
| MCC_CONSUMERS_CONNECTIONS | MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER, CONSUMER_ALTERNATE_CONTACT | Bridges complaints to shared connection and consumer masters. |
| MDM_MCC_COMPTYPE | (Lookup only) | Decode `GRIEVANCETYPEID` before grouping or reporting by complaint type. |
| MDM_MCC_GRIEVSTATUS | (Lookup only) | Decode grievance statuses for human-readable workflow reporting. |

### Validation Samples

- Validated "Show grievances by type and status" with `MCC_GRIEVANCES + MDM_MCC_COMPTYPE + MDM_MCC_GRIEVSTATUS`; March 2025 results returned immediately, led by `Request Services / Solved = 203,094`.
- Validated "Top consumers by complaints" with `MCC_GRIEVANCES -> MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN -> MDM_SHR_CONSUMER`; the join path works and returns named consumers after the large null/blank population.
- Validated "Tanker requests by quarter" with `MCC_GRIEVANCES + MCC_TANKER_RATES`; 2023 quarterly counts returned for all four quarters, confirming tanker lookups belong in scope as MAYBE tables.

## MODULE 2: RBS (Revenue Billing System)

**Status:** Complete

This module covers disconnections, reconnections, billing, collections, water-bill facts, connection ledgers, meter capture, and related workflow/control history. A large share of the RBS family consists of derived reports, seasonal extracts, and temporary repair tables, so the `MAYBE` and `NOT-REQUIRED` buckets are much larger than the canonical core.

### REQUIRED Tables

| Table Name | Row Count | Purpose | Primary Use | Typical Join Targets |

| RBS_BILLADJSTMNTCNTRLRECRD | 1,235,708 | Bill adjustment control history | Track adjustment workflow remarks and approvals | RBS_BILLADJUSTMENT |
| RBS_BILLADJUSTMENT | 1,259,831 | Bill adjustment transaction master | Track credit/debit adjustments by bill period and consumer | RBS_BILLADJSTMNTCNTRLRECRD, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, LKP_RBS_ACTION_TYPE, LKP_RBS_BILL_ADJUSTMENT_TYPE |
| RBS_BILLCOLLECTIONSTMT | 48,009,349 | Bill collection statement header | Analyze collection statements by agency and date | RBS_BILLCOLLSTMTLINEITEM, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER |

### Join Hints for Phase 2 Discovery (RBS Module)

**Use these to batch-query related tables in parallel:**

| Main Table | Typical Join Targets | Why They Are Related |
|---|---|---|
| RBS_DISCONN_REQUEST | RBS_DISCONNECTION_ORDER, RBS_DISCONREQCONREC, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, LKP_DISCONN_STATUS | Full disconnection analysis needs order linkage, workflow history, service connection identity, and decoded status. |
| RBS_CONNECTIONLEDGER_MONTHLY | MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER | Monthly balance analysis needs shared connection and consumer context. |
| RBS_CONNLEDGERTRANS | RBS_CONNECTIONLEDGER, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, LKP_RBS_TRANSACTION_TYPE | Posting-level transaction analysis needs ledger context and transaction-type decoding. |
| RBS_WATERBILL | MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER, MDM_SHR_RBSTARIFFCATEGORIES | Bill facts need service/consumer identity and tariff decoding. |
| RBS_RECONN_REQ | RBS_RECON_ORDER, RBS_REC_REQ_CTRREC, MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN | Reconnection analysis needs request, order, workflow, and service identity together. |

### Validation Samples

- Validated "Disconnections + orders" with `RBS_DISCONN_REQUEST + RBS_DISCONNECTION_ORDER + LKP_DISCONN_STATUS`; 2025 rows returned with linked order counts.
- Validated billing analysis with `RBS_WATERBILL`; January-March 2025 monthly bill counts and net amounts aggregated successfully.
- Validated "Requests by date" with `RBS_DISCONN_REQUEST`; monthly request counts for January-March 2025 returned without needing any excluded table.

## MODULE 3: SHARED / CROSS-MODULE

**Status:** Complete

This section covers shared consumer/service masters, location hierarchy materialized views, lookup tables used across MCC and RBS, and every remaining non-MCC/RBS table in the schema. Only the shared consumer/location/tariff/status core is `REQUIRED`; unrelated module families (HRM, SWC, SUP, IMS, FAS, etc.) are classified `NOT-REQUIRED` for this Phase 1 optimization because they are not part of the complaint/billing/consumer question set.

### REQUIRED Tables

| Table Name | Row Count | Purpose | Primary Use | Typical Join Targets |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CONSUMER_ALTERNATE_CONTACT | 1,507,745 | Alternate contact master | Attach extra mobile numbers to a CAN | MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER |
| LKP_BILLING_MECHANISM | 2 | Billing mechanism lookup | Decode billing mechanism codes | (Lookup table) |
| LKP_CONNECTION_STATUS | 4 | Connection status lookup | Decode connection status codes | (Lookup table) |
| LKP_CONNECTION_TYPE | 4 | Connection type lookup | Decode connection type codes | (Lookup table) |
| LKP_DISCONN_STATUS | 4 | Disconnection status lookup | Decode disconnection request statuses | (Lookup table) |
| LKP_RBS_BILL_ADJUSTMENT_TYPE | 8 | Bill adjustment type lookup | Decode bill adjustment categories | (Lookup table) |
| LKP_RBS_NAMECHANGE_STATUS | 4 | Name change status lookup | Decode name change workflow status | (Lookup table) |
| LKP_RBS_TRANSACTION_TYPE | 65 | RBS transaction type lookup | Decode connection-ledger transaction types | (Lookup table) |
| MCC_SECT_SUBDIVN_DIVN_CIRCLE_INC_AREAS_MV | 6,840 | Area-grain location hierarchy | Decode area, section, subdivision, division, and circle names | (Lookup view; use DISTINCT when joining above AREAID grain) |

### NOT-REQUIRED Tables

| Table Name | Reason Excluded | Category | Classification Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| ADV_ADVICE | Different business module prefix (ADV) outside MCC/RBS/shared consumer scope | Other module | Prefix identifies a separate operational module and the table is not referenced by core complaint, billing, consumer, or location joins. |
| ADV_ADVICECOLLECTIONS | Different business module prefix (ADV) outside MCC/RBS/shared consumer scope | Other module | Prefix identifies a separate operational module and the table is not referenced by core complaint, billing, consumer, or location joins. |
| ADV_ADVINFOITEM | Different business module prefix (ADV) outside MCC/RBS/shared consumer scope | Other module | Prefix identifies a separate operational module and the table is not referenced by core complaint, billing, consumer, or location joins. |
| ADV_ADVSUPPORTDOCS | Different business module prefix (ADV) outside MCC/RBS/shared consumer scope | Other module | Prefix identifies a separate operational module and the table is not referenced by core complaint, billing, consumer, or location joins. |
| ADV_ADVTRANSACTIONITEM | Different business module prefix (ADV) outside MCC/RBS/shared consumer scope | Other module | Prefix identifies a separate operational module and the table is not referenced by core complaint, billing, consumer, or location joins. |

### Join Hints for Phase 2 Discovery (SHARED Module)

**Use these to batch-query related tables in parallel:**

| Main Table | Typical Join Targets | Why They Are Related |
|---|---|---|
| MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN | MDM_SHR_CONSUMER, CONSUMER_ALTERNATE_CONTACT, MDM_SHR_SERVICEACCOUNT, MCC_SECT_SUBDIVN_DIVN_CIRCLE_INC_AREAS_MV, LKP_CONNECTION_STATUS, LKP_CONNECTION_TYPE | This is the universal join hub for service, consumer, location, and status context. |
| MDM_SHR_CONSUMER | MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, CONSUMER_ALTERNATE_CONTACT | Consumer identity and contact analysis depends on both service links and alternate contacts. |
| CONSUMER_ALTERNATE_CONTACT | MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN, MDM_SHR_CONSUMER | Alternate mobile numbers are only meaningful when anchored to shared service and consumer identity. |
| MCC_SECT_SUBDIVN_DIVN_CIRCLE_INC_AREAS_MV | (Lookup view; use DISTINCT above AREAID grain) | This is the standard cross-module location hierarchy view used to decode section/division/circle/area. |
| LKP_CONNECTION_STATUS | (Lookup only) | Required for readable connection-state reporting in both MCC and RBS paths. |

### Validation Samples

- Validated a cross-module consumer lookup with `MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN + MDM_SHR_CONSUMER + CONSUMER_ALTERNATE_CONTACT + LKP_CONNECTION_STATUS + LKP_CONNECTION_TYPE`; sample rows returned live service connections with names and mobile numbers.
- Validated location decoding with `MDM_SHR_SERVICECONN + MCC_SECT_SUBDIVN_DIVN_CIRCLE_INC_AREAS_MV`; top circles for 2025 Q1 connection generation returned as expected.
- The large NOT-REQUIRED population in this module is intentional: those tables belong to unrelated operational domains or clearly non-canonical temp/backup/audit families and are not needed for Phase 1 query optimization.

---

## Key Classification Signals Used

- Naming patterns: temp/staging, backup/snapshot, audit/log, test/bugfix, and unrelated module prefixes were treated as strong exclusion signals.
- Row counts: zero-row tables were not auto-excluded; they were kept as `MAYBE` if they looked like legitimate future/shared lookups and excluded only when multiple signals agreed.
- Canonical source preference: live transactional/master tables were preferred over reports, snapshots, duplicate copies, and denormalized extracts.
- Join-path relevance: `REQUIRED` tables are exactly the ones needed to support working MCC, RBS, and shared validation queries plus their decoding lookups.
- Conservative fallback: when a table looked business-relevant but not universally needed, it stayed in `MAYBE` rather than being over-excluded.

## Phase 1 Exit Criteria Check

- All 2,470 tables owned by `CTLHMWSSBTMP` were classified into `REQUIRED`, `MAYBE`, or `NOT-REQUIRED`.
- Every `REQUIRED` table documents typical join targets for Phase 2 parallel discovery.
- Every `NOT-REQUIRED` row includes a reason, category, and evidence statement.
- Validation queries passed for MCC, RBS, and shared/cross-module join paths.
- The file is now in the expected 3-module + summary format for downstream phases.
